# LedgerForge Read-Only Discovery Register

**Discovery date:** 2026-09-10. **Immutable research ref:** `adcf83f52d309ddac18d95f0321d0c0f6120dd29`.
**Publication input ref:** `ffddebc6e12405c812a36855ab6212e973cff5ba`. **Writer window:** ACKNOWLEDGED — Sprint83 owner release; next campaign holds with no frozen parent. **Independent review:** Sol High PASS; Luna Max mechanical checkpoint; primary final staged static audit PASS.

This unnumbered register records research and proposals. FUTURE_WORK remains the actionable queue; the current roadmap owns numbering, Chat owns architecture and sprint acceptance, and this publication grants no implementation authority. Every current-behavior claim below is bounded to the examined source, not a new executable validation. All proposed boundaries and personal-v1 relevance assessments remain proposals unless an explicit user decision is identified.

The pinned queue contained **153** unique entries: P0 12, P1 37, P2 60, P3 44. At publication there are **151** active entries and **2 separately completed Sprint 83 entries**, retained here only as baseline history.
The pinned status counts are Ready for discovery 13, Research 36, Blocked 51, Candidate 36, Ready for planning 8, Idea 4, Maintenance 5. All 149 non-Idea entries have substantive results, reused evidence, active-owner coverage or exact named blockers. Three Ideas receive relevance triage only and are not described as completed research; the fourth has an explicit evidence result. No priority or active candidate status is promoted by this campaign.

Result types: `NAMED_BLOCKER` 85, `REUSED_EVIDENCE` 19, `SUBSTANTIVE_RESULT` 46, `TRIAGE_ONLY` 3. Dispositions: `BLOCKED_BY_NAMED_DEPENDENCY` 57, `DEFERRED_WITH_EXPLICIT_REASON` 5, `DISCOVER_NOW` 28, `NEEDS_SOURCE_OR_USER_DECISION` 43, `OWNED_BY_ACTIVE_SPRINT` 6, `REUSE_EXISTING_DISCOVERY` 14.

## Authority, method and handover

Astra Max led integration; Terra High readers divided integrity, sources, macOS and workflow work; Sol High independently challenged consequential financial/recovery proposals; Luna Max supplied a mechanical audit checkpoint; the primary completed final staged inventory, link, hash, range and membership verification. Requested routes were checked against runtime metadata. The user removed numerical lane caps; the host exposed four concurrent slots including the primary. Only the primary may write the five authorized documentation paths during the acknowledged window.
Research used a read-only archive of the exact pushed commit. No product implementation, build, TestPlan, corpus campaign, app/database mutation, source-original modification, branch or linked worktree was performed. Existing tests are cited as definitions or accepted historical evidence, never as newly executed results. No benchmark or native accessibility result was produced.
At the research ref, committed acceptance documents ended at Sprint 82 although Sprint 83 product bytes were pushed. Separate Chat acceptance and the Sprint 83 documentation owner subsequently control that transition; this campaign neither accepts Sprint 83 nor reopens its implementation. Sprints 84–87 remain separately owned, and no future parent may be frozen before the documentation handover is returned.
The complete authentic corpus remains authority for any future statement-dependent acceptance under ADR-046. Synthetic/sanitized/reconstructed statements or statement-shaped domain/DTO graphs are prohibited at every stage. Source-independent nonfinancial mechanics may use isolated values. Absent authentic zero-activity or CBQ history-XLS cases remain source-uncertified. Existing accepted family evidence never establishes new institution-wide support.

## Cross-cutting results and explicit decisions

- **Integrity:** no new financial defect was reproduced by this read-only campaign. Historical repair/reversal needs one independently provable family, a consistent reviewed snapshot, exact shared-provenance impact and family-specific correction/reversal semantics. Existing duplicate rejection is not proof of historical corruption.
- **R1:** appearance needs a preference-ownership decision; visual approval does not substitute for future native keyboard, VoiceOver, resize, contrast and reduced-transparency acceptance. App-shell decomposition and physical source-tree organization remain separate boundaries.
- **Transfers/rules/planning:** propose reviewable same-currency one-to-one transfer evidence and explainable read-only categorization proposals first. Separate source facts, user assumptions, derived results and forecasts. Prepared 97 needs a coherence decision before combination.
- **Investment direction — EXPLICIT_USER_DECISION, 2026-09-10:** “Propose current holdings first; keep history and performance gated.” This approves the proposed planning direction only. Container/instrument identity, exact owned units and explicit cost-evidence type precede valuation; no investment sprint or architecture is selected.
- **Recovery/export:** existing DEBUG lifecycle backup is a reusable mechanism, not an accepted user-owned backup product. Propose isolated verified restoration before activation, plus separate versioned domain export and selected-view export contracts. Adoption requires the exact Chat-designated pre-v1 scope, not every vision item.
- **Optional ecosystem:** defer sync, public APIs, external plugins and cross-platform execution until a concrete owner workflow justifies their key recovery, conflict, trust and maintenance cost. Research dispositions preserve their queue entries.

## Workbook and public-source evidence

The owner-authorized Budget Analysis workbook was read through the connected Sheets interface on 2026-09-10; the observed workbook modification timestamp was 2026-09-09T15:40:46.306Z. This is private workflow evidence, not an accepted financial oracle. No private financial values, identifiers, names, transaction text or raw workbook extracts are published here. The inspected scope was Dashboard planning/totals, Savings / Investments position formulas, Monthly ISP balance update structure and the credit-card analysis header. Hidden fund-source sheet contents/custom-function code and complete transaction history were not inspected.
Indian-fund rows use entered units and a cost-like total while stock/ETF rows derive cost from units times entered average unit price. ISP rows keep mandatory/AVC units and contribution-allocation formulas; allocated contribution cost is not inferred tax-lot history. Dashboard totals include remittance within a workbook expense-labelled range, so that label cannot become spending semantics. Current gain percentages do not prove time-weighted or money-weighted performance. Retired rate haircuts and the historical ISP 0.75 multiplier stay retired; no replacement vesting model is invented.

Primary-observed, privacy-safe workbook observation record **WB-20260910-A** (2026-09-10). The cells below were read as entered/effective values and formulas; only their field roles are retained. These are direct local observations, independently of repository evidence, and are not financial truth certification.

| Observation | Exact inspected sheet/range | Privacy-safe observed formula/field role |
|---|---|---|
| <a id="wb1"></a>WB1 | Dashboard A1:V35 and A82:P88 | Earnings minus deductions is a formula; expense-labelled totals include the India-transfer column. Current-month prediction combines entered balances/obligations with a fixed referenced earnings row. External-rate formulas include retired haircut assumptions. |
| <a id="wb2"></a>WB2 | Savings / Investments B12:K26 | Entered units E and cost-like total F; current value H is E multiplied by fetched price D; gain I subtracts F, despite the Average price column heading. Dates/names/prices are lookup formulas. |
| <a id="wb3"></a>WB3 | Savings / Investments B27:K41 | Entered fractional units E and average unit price F; total cost G is E multiplied by F; value H is E multiplied by fetched price D; gain I subtracts G. This differs from WB2 cost meaning. |
| <a id="wb4"></a>WB4 | Monthly ISP balance update A1:J18; Savings / Investments B8:K11 | Mandatory and AVC unit columns G/H sum to D; contribution totals are allocated by entered percentages into E. Separate entered observations and allocation ratios do not prove lots, transaction history, vesting or provider price dates. |
| <a id="wb5"></a>WB5 | Savings / Investments G46:H47 | Totals combine costs/values and cash across converted categories using Dashboard reference-rate formulas. They are workflow calculations, not an accepted historical-rate or net-worth oracle. |

| Source, observed 2026-09-10 | Established fact and remaining boundary |
|---|---|
| [User-supplied FE/Zurich Fund Centre](https://digital.feprecisionplus.com/zilpricingtable?performancecurrency=null&category=126&MPCategoryCode=126) | Footer identifies valuation-date prices used for unit transactions on the following working day. Parsed headers supplied no per-row date or Change-% period. Copying notice does not establish permission to automate and retain a persistent cache. The completed 2026-09-09 price retrieval was not replayed; its exact code/currency findings remain recorded prior discovery. |
| [AMFI terms](https://www.amfiindia.com/terms-of-use) | Personal/noncommercial use and storage restrictions require precise provider/cache qualification. Prior NAVAll identity/feed discovery is reused; exact configured code + ISIN + Direct plan/option and separately qualified expected INR remain necessary. |
| [ExchangeRate-API open data documentation](https://www.exchangerate-api.com/docs/free) and [terms](https://www.exchangerate-api.com/terms) | Open endpoint documents daily refresh, attribution and end-use caching, with redistribution restrictions and informational suitability. It remains a technically qualified candidate, not an accepted provider. General INR → QAR → USD observations retain both source legs, timestamps, digest/version and explicit stale/unavailable states. Al Dar stays with Salary/remittance. |
| [SQLite online backup](https://www.sqlite.org/backup.html) and [WAL documentation](https://www.sqlite.org/wal.html) | Snapshot backup and same-host WAL coordination differ from copying a live database into arbitrary synchronization software. Backup, portable package, sync and restoration each need their own accepted boundary. |
| [Apple FileVault volume encryption](https://support.apple.com/en-euro/guide/security/sec4c6dc1b6e/web) | Volume protection and application/export encryption address different artifact/threat boundaries. No local FileVault state was inspected. No DB-encryption or key-recovery architecture is selected. |

The exact ISP page supplied by the user is retained above for continuation under FW-P3-27/FW-P3-29. `PRICE_DATE_NOT_ESTABLISHED` still applies to the actual per-fund observation, despite the footer clarification. No performance date is repurposed as a price date. Prices never establish owned units.

## Unnumbered research queue routing

These residual research bullets remain unpromoted research, not new implementation candidates. Parser assistance/ambiguous-layout recovery and cross-format shared evidence route to FW-P1-06/07/08/17 and remain gated by exact authentic-source, independent oracle and privacy/review decisions. Mutation-history compaction routes to FW-P0-16 only after an actual durable mutation family and retention/reversal contract exist. Explainable recommendations route to FW-P3-06, anomaly work to FW-P2-28, and tax/regulatory assistance to FW-P3-16; domain/policy/source gaps in those cards apply. No duplicate IDs or sprint numbers are allocated.

## Discovery cards

<a id="fw-p0-02"></a>
### FW-P0-02 — Historical Duplicate Review and Repair

**Baseline:** P0; Ready for discovery. **Disposition:** `DISCOVER_NOW`. **Result:** `SUBSTANTIVE_RESULT`. **Research owner:** Terra integrity.
**Examined ref/date:** `adcf83f52d309ddac18d95f0321d0c0f6120dd29` / 2026-09-10. **Delta at `ffddebc6e12405c812a36855ab6212e973cff5ba`:** `REVALIDATED_AFTER_CHANGE` — Latest changed document inputs compared with pinned evidence; accepted Sprint 83 closure is preserved. Production source/test inputs are unchanged.

**Recorded queue dependencies/gates at baseline:** **Shared prerequisites:** Sprint 40 overlap evidence, Sprint 41 bounded transaction-event ownership, ADR-037 acceptance and post-Sprint-45 lifecycle revalidation are complete. **Implementation dependency:** Selection and approval of one concrete historical duplicate-repair family contract. Targeted discovery may now define one bounded repair family, including: - independently provable duplicate evidence; - import-session and source provenance; Historical data must never be silently changed. Discovery does not authorize implementation or broad historical cleanup. **May combine with:** `FW-P1-25` and `FW-P2-12` only if discovery proves that prospective duplicate review and one historical repair family share a single bounded review and acceptance boundary.
**Original FW references:** `FW-P1-25`, `FW-P2-12`

- **Question:** Choose one historical duplicate repair family without altering history by inference.
- **Behavior / evidence classification at research ref:** VERIFIED_REPOSITORY: exact fingerprint and supported event duplicate outcomes reject the incoming import and retain a bounded attempt; no historical repair API exists.
- **Accepted constraints:** ADR-037 requires read-only review, exact native-currency impact, confirmation, provider-owned atomic write/audit and family reversal or declared irreversibility.
- **Missing evidence or decision:** MISSING: an affected family/database and independent duplicate oracle; static absence is not a reproduced defect.
- **Options and tradeoffs:** PROPOSED: select one independently source-provable duplicate relation; defer broad cleanup. A generic candidate scan cannot itself authorize repair.
- **Smallest proposed boundary:** PROPOSED read-only historical duplicate verifier first; only then a separately approved one-family repair plan.
- **Scope and exclusions:** No fuzzy matching, silent edits, bulk cleanup, synthetic-source proof or backup-as-undo.
- **Dependencies and decisions:** Selected family, authoritative snapshot, independent evidence, ADR-037 executor prerequisites.
- **ADR and migration implications:** ADR-037 applies; migration unknown and not allocated.
- **Falsifiable future acceptance / independent oracle:** Falsify with ambiguous evidence, stale plan, differing provider result, or any accepted residue on rejected review; prove independent source impact and post-hydration/relaunch.
- **Invalidators / revalidation trigger:** A source-proven family whose records are not representable by the reviewed contract.
- **Personal-v1 relevance:** PROPOSED pre-v1 relevance unresolved; no certification claim.
- **Queue and roadmap recommendation:** DEFERRED_WITH_REASON — retain Ready for discovery; roadmap correctly says one family remains unselected.
- **Evidence anchors:** [E028](#e028) — FW-P0-02 (baseline lines 254-276); [E037](#e037) — exactDuplicate (baseline lines 1857-1863).

<a id="fw-p0-08"></a>
### FW-P0-08 — Data-Integrity Verification and Repair

**Baseline:** P0; Ready for discovery. **Disposition:** `DISCOVER_NOW`. **Result:** `NAMED_BLOCKER`. **Research owner:** Terra integrity.
**Examined ref/date:** `adcf83f52d309ddac18d95f0321d0c0f6120dd29` / 2026-09-10. **Delta at `ffddebc6e12405c812a36855ab6212e973cff5ba`:** `REVALIDATED_AFTER_CHANGE` — Latest changed document inputs compared with pinned evidence; accepted Sprint 83 closure is preserved. Production source/test inputs are unchanged.

**Recorded queue dependencies/gates at baseline:** **Shared prerequisites:** ADR-037 acceptance and post-Sprint-45 lifecycle revalidation are complete. **Implementation dependency:** Selection and approval of one concrete verification or repair family. The Axis direction-audit family has no retained affected historical database currently identified. No detection or repair run is presently authorized. If a concrete affected database is supplied, the subfamily must first perform read-only detection against durable parser provenance and independently provable source arithmetic; any repair then requires exact impact preview, explicit authorization, provider-owned atomic mutation and recoverability or explicit irreversibility. The Sprint 55A source-truth restoration performed no historical mutation. Targeted discovery must select one independently verifiable family and define whether the first increment is read-only verification or an explicit repair. Repair actions must be previewed, authorized, atomic and recoverable or explicitly irreversible.
**Original FW references:** None recorded.

- **Question:** Select a safe first integrity verification/repair family.
- **Behavior / evidence classification at research ref:** VERIFIED_REPOSITORY: provider rejects malformed/identity/event conflicts; repository protocol contains no cross-domain repair transaction.
- **Accepted constraints:** The Axis direction audit has no identified affected database; read-only detection must use provenance and independent source arithmetic before a repair is considered.
- **Missing evidence or decision:** MISSING: concrete affected database/family and a consistent approved snapshot.
- **Options and tradeoffs:** PROPOSED: begin with a read-only verifier for one invariant (for example complete import graph), rather than repair-everything.
- **Smallest proposed boundary:** PROPOSED verification report with no writes and privacy-safe counts/relationships.
- **Scope and exclusions:** No affected-DB mutation, scan of live DB, broad repair or invented historical oracle.
- **Dependencies and decisions:** Exact supplied snapshot, selected invariant, independent oracle; any repair additionally depends on ADR-037 family contract.
- **ADR and migration implications:** ADR-037; no migration decision.
- **Falsifiable future acceptance / independent oracle:** Known malformed graph must be detected; valid graph passes; unreadable/missing evidence fails closed; verifier produces no state change.
- **Invalidators / revalidation trigger:** A supplied database proves the chosen invariant cannot be evaluated from durable data/provenance.
- **Personal-v1 relevance:** PROPOSED important recoverability input, not certification.
- **Queue and roadmap recommendation:** DEFERRED_WITH_REASON — retain Ready for discovery with named affected-database blocker.
- **Evidence anchors:** [E028](#e028) — FW-P0-08 (baseline lines 280-300); [E006](#e006) — ConfirmedImportRepository (baseline lines 279-288).

<a id="fw-p0-11"></a>
### FW-P0-11 — Manual Account Linking and Unlinking

**Baseline:** P0; Blocked. **Disposition:** `NEEDS_SOURCE_OR_USER_DECISION`. **Result:** `NAMED_BLOCKER`. **Research owner:** Terra integrity.
**Examined ref/date:** `adcf83f52d309ddac18d95f0321d0c0f6120dd29` / 2026-09-10. **Delta at `ffddebc6e12405c812a36855ab6212e973cff5ba`:** `REVALIDATED_AFTER_CHANGE` — Latest changed document inputs compared with pinned evidence; accepted Sprint 83 closure is preserved. Production source/test inputs are unchanged.

**Recorded queue dependencies/gates at baseline:** **Depends on:** Accepted ADR-037 and independently approved linking, unlinking and identifier-detachment semantics The account-outcome explanation prerequisite was completed by Sprint 61. The remaining blocker is independently approved linking, unlinking and identifier-detachment semantics under ADR-037; this candidate is not implementation-ready. - no identity decisions based solely on filenames, labels or display metadata.
**Original FW references:** None recorded.

- **Question:** Define manual link/unlink and identifier-detachment semantics.
- **Behavior / evidence classification at research ref:** VERIFIED_REPOSITORY: identifiers attach and resolve inside confirmed import; no detach/move endpoint is exposed.
- **Accepted constraints:** Immutable account identity, explicit confirmation, impact preview, provenance preservation; labels, filenames and weak metadata are not identity authority.
- **Missing evidence or decision:** EXPLICIT_USER_DECISION: eligible link proof, detach effect and reversal/irreversibility.
- **Options and tradeoffs:** PROPOSED define one detach-only family before a link/move family; merge neither with import choice.
- **Smallest proposed boundary:** PROPOSED read-only relationship preview contract.
- **Scope and exclusions:** No automatic linking, identifier reassignment, account merge/split or runtime-store authority.
- **Dependencies and decisions:** Approved semantics and ADR-037 mutation family.
- **ADR and migration implications:** ADR-037; possible relation/audit schema is unknown.
- **Falsifiable future acceptance / independent oracle:** Conflicting/weak proof rejects; plan enumerates affected identifiers, sessions and transactions; provider parity/hydration holds.
- **Invalidators / revalidation trigger:** Any contract that needs source identity reconstructed from weak metadata.
- **Personal-v1 relevance:** PROPOSED pre-v1 only if user adopts identity correction as a gate.
- **Queue and roadmap recommendation:** DEFERRED_WITH_REASON — preserve Blocked.
- **Evidence anchors:** [E028](#e028) — FW-P0-11 (baseline lines 306-322); [E006](#e006) — AccountRepository (baseline lines 228-237).

<a id="fw-p0-12"></a>
### FW-P0-12 — Identifier Backfill Policy

**Baseline:** P0; Blocked. **Disposition:** `REUSE_EXISTING_DISCOVERY`. **Result:** `REUSED_EVIDENCE`. **Research owner:** Terra integrity.
**Examined ref/date:** `adcf83f52d309ddac18d95f0321d0c0f6120dd29` / 2026-09-10. **Delta at `ffddebc6e12405c812a36855ab6212e973cff5ba`:** `REVALIDATED_AFTER_CHANGE` — Latest changed document inputs compared with pinned evidence; accepted Sprint 83 closure is preserved. Production source/test inputs are unchanged.

**Recorded queue dependencies/gates at baseline:** **Architecture:** ADR-038 accepted in Sprint 49 **Depends on:** Separate future decisions for identifier correction, reassignment and historical repair Sprint 49 approved the confirmed-import portion of identifier enrichment. When parser-produced strong verified identifiers uniquely resolve one existing account, existing same-account identifiers are idempotently accepted and additional eligible identifiers may be attached automatically only when each is unowned or already owned by that resolved account. Conflicting ownership rejects the complete import, and attachment occurs only as part of a successful provider-owned atomic confirmed import. Sprint 50 implemented durable identifier ownership and accepted-import observation provenance for successful confirmed imports. Each bounded observation relates identifier ownership to its accepted import session, accepted document, parser provenance and account-association authority; transaction-time ownership enforcement is active, and historical observations are not invented. The following work remains open and separately gated: The completed confirmed-import concurrency slice and this enrichment slice were combined only because they share one atomic confirmation boundary. Identifier-management workflows outside successful confirmed import do not become part of that operation and remain independently governed.
**Original FW references:** None recorded.

- **Question:** What identifier backfill remains after confirmed-import enrichment?
- **Behavior / evidence classification at research ref:** VERIFIED_REPOSITORY: atomic confirmed import permits only unowned/same-owner strong identifiers and records observations; conflicting owners reject complete import.
- **Accepted constraints:** Historical observations are not invented; noMatch requires explicit eligible account choice and transaction-time revalidation.
- **Missing evidence or decision:** MISSING separate policies for correction, reassignment, unlinking and historical repair.
- **Options and tradeoffs:** PROPOSED keep accepted enrichment separate from later identity repair.
- **Smallest proposed boundary:** PROPOSED correction policy only after unlink semantics.
- **Scope and exclusions:** No backfilled observations or automatic reassignment.
- **Dependencies and decisions:** FW-P0-11 and a repair family decision.
- **ADR and migration implications:** ADR-038 stays; later audit/relation decision unknown.
- **Falsifiable future acceptance / independent oracle:** Provider rejects cross-owner candidate with zero accepted graph; historical data remains unchanged.
- **Invalidators / revalidation trigger:** Evidence that a proposed identifier is not parser-produced strong verified evidence.
- **Personal-v1 relevance:** Settled: current enrichment accepted; broader policy is not certification evidence.
- **Queue and roadmap recommendation:** DEFERRED_WITH_REASON — preserve Blocked and completed slice wording.
- **Evidence anchors:** [E028](#e028) — FW-P0-12 (baseline lines 326-347); [E007](#e007) — commitInsideTransaction (baseline lines 182-224).

**Baseline:** P0; Blocked. **Disposition:** `BLOCKED_BY_NAMED_DEPENDENCY`. **Result:** `NAMED_BLOCKER`. **Research owner:** Terra integrity.
**Examined ref/date:** `adcf83f52d309ddac18d95f0321d0c0f6120dd29` / 2026-09-10. **Delta at `ffddebc6e12405c812a36855ab6212e973cff5ba`:** `REVALIDATED_AFTER_CHANGE` — Latest changed document inputs compared with pinned evidence; accepted Sprint 83 closure is preserved. **Recorded queue dependencies/gates at baseline:** **Discovery:** Complete **Depends on:** An approved `FW-P3-40` multiple-workspace architecture that explicitly decides fingerprint scope Define exact-statement fingerprint ownership when one LedgerForge database contains more than one independent workspace. - treatment of identical source content imported into independent workspaces; Fingerprint scope is a subdecision of the future multiple-workspace architecture. `FW-P0-18` must not be treated as an independent prerequisite that blocks discovery of `FW-P3-40`, because doing so creates a circular dependency. The source digest must remain derived from source evidence rather than being altered merely to encode workspace identity.
**Original FW references:** `FW-P0-18`, `FW-P3-40`

- **Question:** Should exact-source fingerprint uniqueness be workspace or database scoped?
- **Behavior / evidence classification at research ref:** VERIFIED_REPOSITORY: current duplicate authority is queried by algorithm/fingerprint without workspace in the visible confirmed-import check.
- **Accepted constraints:** Digest stays source-derived; fingerprint scope is a subdecision of FW-P3-40, avoiding circular blockage.
- **Missing evidence or decision:** EXPLICIT_USER_DECISION multiple-workspace identity/isolation/migration semantics.
- **Options and tradeoffs:** PROPOSED let workspace architecture select scope then design transition; do not salt digest.
- **Smallest proposed boundary:** PROPOSED architecture decision record only.
- **Scope and exclusions:** No schema change or workspace implementation.
- **Dependencies and decisions:** FW-P3-40 architecture.
- **ADR and migration implications:** Migration required only after scope decision; no number.
- **Falsifiable future acceptance / independent oracle:** Identical source in two isolated workspaces follows selected rule and privacy constraints; existing DB transition is explicit.
- **Invalidators / revalidation trigger:** Chosen workspace design cannot preserve source-derived digest.
- **Personal-v1 relevance:** Unsettled, likely post-v1 multiple-workspace feature.
- **Queue and roadmap recommendation:** DEFERRED_WITH_REASON — retain Blocked; do not block FW-P3-40 discovery.

<a id="fw-p2-01"></a>
### FW-P2-01 — Source-Document Reopening

**Baseline:** P2; Candidate. **Disposition:** `NEEDS_SOURCE_OR_USER_DECISION`. **Result:** `NAMED_BLOCKER`. **Research owner:** Terra workflow.
**Examined ref/date:** `adcf83f52d309ddac18d95f0321d0c0f6120dd29` / 2026-09-10. **Delta at `ffddebc6e12405c812a36855ab6212e973cff5ba`:** `REVALIDATED_AFTER_CHANGE` — Latest changed document inputs compared with pinned evidence; accepted Sprint 83 closure is preserved. Production source/test inputs are unchanged.

**Recorded queue dependencies/gates at baseline:** **Depends on:** Approved document-library and source-retention architecture Sprint 67A corrected the completed read-only transaction-detail presentation so source-document metadata is bound to the transaction's exact durable imported-document record; import-session display labels are not document authority. Only source-document reopening, browsing or library behavior remains here. It requires a separately approved architecture and privacy boundary; filenames, raw source bytes, paths and display labels must not become durable identity.
**Original FW references:** None recorded.

- **Question:** Source-Document Reopening: establish the smallest truthful product boundary.
- **Behavior / evidence classification at research ref:** VERIFIED_REPOSITORY: transaction detail presents a durable preferred/source document name and session-backed provenance, while DocumentStore retains only process-local text rows and transactions. Neither establishes retained original bytes, a re-openable security-scoped URL, or availability of an original after import.
- **Accepted constraints:** EXPLICIT_USER_DECISION: preserve immutable imported financial truth, durable provenance, native-currency meaning, privacy, deterministic behavior, and fail-closed ambiguity; a roadmap forecast grants no execution authority.
- **Missing evidence or decision:** EXPLICIT_USER_DECISION: choose one reopening contract. A: user re-authorizes the exact original; fingerprint match proves it is the same retained source before opening. B: LedgerForge retains an approved source copy under an explicit encryption/privacy, storage, deletion, migration and recovery contract. C: library metadata only; this is FW-P2-54 and does not implement reopening. MISSING: availability of any original is never assumed.
- **Options and tradeoffs:** PROPOSED: A is least-retention and requires per-open user selection plus exact fingerprint comparison; B gives offline reopening but adds sensitive-content retention/recovery obligations; C is metadata-only navigation and is lower risk but cannot reopen. Do not conflate C with an answer to FW-P2-01.
- **Smallest proposed boundary:** PROPOSED: Chat select A, B, or C before implementation. If A, test a same-fingerprint reauthorization and reject mismatch/unavailable original without opening; if B, separately approve retention lifecycle; if C, route work to FW-P2-54 only.
- **Scope and exclusions:** No repair, parser/source claim, build/test/runtime validation, database mutation, fabricated statement, unbounded sync, or hidden financial inference.
- **Dependencies and decisions:** Document-library architecture..
- **ADR and migration implications:** No migration or ADR is approved by discovery; any durable relation/derived-state schema requires a selected family and explicit architecture decision.
- **Falsifiable future acceptance / independent oracle:** A: independent original fingerprint equals durable authority before presentation, while a mismatch/missing file opens nothing and changes no history. B: retention, deletion and restore behavior is proven against an approved privacy contract. C: navigator shows only authoritative safe metadata and never claims content availability. All paths preserve original source values and show provenance limitations.
- **Invalidators / revalidation trigger:** An original whose fingerprint cannot be compared, a retention requirement without privacy/recovery decision, or metadata displayed as proof that a reopenable source exists invalidates the boundary.
- **Personal-v1 relevance:** PROPOSED: no card is personal-v1 certification evidence without separately accepted scope and certification gate.
- **Queue and roadmap recommendation:** DEFERRED_WITH_REASON — preserve status and named blocker; do not select/reorder a sprint.
- **Evidence anchors:** [E028](#e028) — FW-P2-01 (baseline lines 1113-1185); [E031](#e031) — prepared roadmap (baseline lines 19-123); [E039](#e039) — detailPresentation(for:) (baseline lines 120-209); [E003](#e003) — DocumentStore (baseline lines 16-48).

<a id="fw-p2-54"></a>
### FW-P2-54 — Document Library and Source-Document Browsing

**Baseline:** P2; Ready for discovery. **Disposition:** `DISCOVER_NOW`. **Result:** `SUBSTANTIVE_RESULT`. **Research owner:** Terra workflow.
**Examined ref/date:** `adcf83f52d309ddac18d95f0321d0c0f6120dd29` / 2026-09-10. **Delta at `ffddebc6e12405c812a36855ab6212e973cff5ba`:** `REVALIDATED_AFTER_CHANGE` — Latest changed document inputs compared with pinned evidence; accepted Sprint 83 closure is preserved. Production source/test inputs are unchanged.

**Recorded queue dependencies/gates at baseline:** **Shared prerequisites:** Durable document provenance, transaction-to-source relationships and import-attempt history now exist. **Implementation dependency:** Approval of the privacy-safe metadata, navigation and retention boundary. Provide a user-facing place to inspect imported source-document records and their relationships to: Targeted discovery must determine: - how document identity differs from filename and source-content identity; - whether source reopening or content retention is excluded; The library must not expose raw source content by default and must not treat a document record as the owner of account identity.
**Original FW references:** None recorded.

- **Question:** Document Library and Source-Document Browsing: establish the smallest truthful product boundary.
- **Behavior / evidence classification at research ref:** VERIFIED_REPOSITORY: transaction detail derives a privacy-bounded source-document name from durable transaction provenance and exposes session validation status; repository contracts expose import session/document and partial/equivalence read models. This is sufficient evidence for metadata relationship navigation, not for reopening or raw-content retention.
- **Accepted constraints:** EXPLICIT_USER_DECISION: preserve immutable imported financial truth, durable provenance, native-currency meaning, privacy, deterministic behavior, and fail-closed ambiguity; a roadmap forecast grants no execution authority.
- **Missing evidence or decision:** MISSING/EXPLICIT_USER_DECISION: Privacy-safe authoritative metadata, retention, navigation, duplicate/partial/legacy handling and reopening exclusion.
- **Options and tradeoffs:** PROPOSED: metadata-only library (safe document label, source format/profile where retained, period/count/outcome, account/session/transaction relations) is independently useful. Retained source copy and re-open original are separate options owned by FW-P2-01; path/raw-byte search/export are separate privacy decisions.
- **Smallest proposed boundary:** PROPOSED: read-only library list/detail plus account/session/transaction navigation. Exact duplicate points to prior outcome; partial/unsupported/legacy gaps remain explicit; library never claims the original is available.
- **Scope and exclusions:** No repair, parser/source claim, build/test/runtime validation, database mutation, fabricated statement, unbounded sync, or hidden financial inference.
- **Dependencies and decisions:** Approved metadata/navigation/retention boundary..
- **ADR and migration implications:** No migration or ADR is approved by discovery; any durable relation/derived-state schema requires a selected family and explicit architecture decision.
- **Falsifiable future acceptance / independent oracle:** A hydrated document relationship is navigable from each permitted entry point and is rejected/labelled unavailable if document/session/workspace provenance is inconsistent. Exact duplicate and partial attempt routes are distinguishable; no raw path, bytes, credential, hidden identifier or reopen control appears. The feature has zero financial writes.
- **Invalidators / revalidation trigger:** Missing provenance, mixed-currency total, weak identity/recurrence evidence, changed source contract, or a result that cannot distinguish fact from derived suggestion invalidates this recommendation.
- **Personal-v1 relevance:** PROPOSED: no card is personal-v1 certification evidence without separately accepted scope and certification gate.
- **Queue and roadmap recommendation:** DEFERRED_WITH_REASON — discovery result supports a bounded later decision; preserve current status.

Those slices are not future work. **Remaining boundary:** Broader cross-employer planning integration, historical planner analytics/variance explanation and other planning outcomes that genuinely extend the accepted current-month workspace. Generic employer source support stays under `FW-P1-05`; recurring obligations under `FW-P3-09`; transfer/reconciliation under `FW-P2-10`/`FW-P2-11`/`FW-P3-19`; investments and general reporting FX remain in their dedicated candidates. **User-settled future India-remittance reference direction — 2026-09-09:** Al Dar Exchange is prospective evidence only for **Salary / This Month / India-remittance planning**. It is not general FX, investment FX, ISP FX, net-worth FX or reporting-currency authority. Read-only public-calculator discovery established amount-sensitive forward QAR-send quoting through the provider's public calculator path and also established that the reverse INR-target result is not reciprocal enough to prove sufficient funding. The reverse endpoint may inform later implementation investigation but is not financial authority. Current production remains the explicit dated **user-entered** planning FX contract. A future external quote must retain distinct provider/source/amount/direction/fetch provenance and must never be mislabeled `user_entered`. Manual user-entered FX remains a supported fallback/override. External Al Dar use requires a future explicit ADR-045 alignment or successor architecture before it can affect Salary calculations. The transfer fee remains a separate editable user/planning input. Public calculator discovery did not establish a universal applicable fee, so LedgerForge must not auto-fetch or auto-change the configured fee without separate evidence. The current accepted configurable-fee behavior remains unchanged. Further Salary visual polish remains lower priority and must not reopen accepted editor or persistence behavior.

The current selected-view export item has no accepted report schema/privacy policy; repository rows alone do not define the visible filter, ordering or displayed totals contract.
- **Accepted constraints:** The app remains private, local and explicit. Accepted financial history, native Money, order, multiplicity and provenance cannot be silently rewritten. ADR-046 authentic-input rules apply to financial cases; migration identities remain append-only. Discovery grants no implementation or acceptance.

- **Falsifiable future acceptance / independent oracle:** Independently compare membership, native Money and ordering against genuine canonical records; verify commas/newlines/Unicode and formula-like nonfinancial strings, cancellation/no partial final artifact, and empty results. Do not manufacture financial statement test graphs.
- **Invalidators / revalidation trigger:** Changed filters, paginated membership, new financial columns or provider rounding can invalidate the chosen schema.
- **Personal-v1 relevance:** A structured/user export boundary is required by FW-P0-26, but Chat must choose the exact adopted reports.

<a id="fw-p3-20"></a>
### FW-P3-20 — Mutual-Fund Holdings

**Baseline:** P3; Research. **Disposition:** `NEEDS_SOURCE_OR_USER_DECISION`. **Result:** `SUBSTANTIVE_RESULT`. **Research owner:** Astra primary.
**Examined ref/date:** `adcf83f52d309ddac18d95f0321d0c0f6120dd29` / 2026-09-10. **Delta at `ffddebc6e12405c812a36855ab6212e973cff5ba`:** `STILL_VALID` — Cited input hashes unchanged.

**Recorded queue dependencies/gates at baseline:** **Depends on:** accepted investment/holding identity and valuation provenance; source/user-owned position and cost evidence; authentic investment sources where ingestion support is selected Holding ownership, units, contributions/cost basis and correction history remain source/user-owned investment evidence. A fetched NAV never establishes those facts. External AMFI NAV retrieval/freshness belongs under `FW-P3-27`; manual/custom valuation fallback belongs under `FW-P3-28` only when required. Any later reporting conversion is a separate provenance-bearing derived layer and never overwrites the native holding value or imported/source evidence.
**Original FW references:** `FW-P3-27`, `FW-P3-28`

- **Question:** What is the smallest Indian mutual-fund holding boundary?
- **Behavior / evidence classification at research ref:** VERIFIED_REPOSITORY: AccountType.investment and optional base-currency fields exist, but the inspected repository interfaces contain no holding/security/lot or reference-rate service. The exchange_rates table is historical schema capacity, not an implemented FX product. Money rejects excess currency precision. VERIFIED_LOCAL: Budget Analysis / Savings / Investments B12:J26 uses fetched display/date/price formulas, entered units and a cost-like entered amount, and derives value from units times price; it supplies neither AMFI mapping nor transaction history.
- **Accepted constraints:** ADR-008/033 preserve native Money and require separately approved derived rounding/conversion. ADR-046 governs every statement-dependent path. Source/user holding facts, external observations and derived values have separate authority; no invented units, cost, identifiers, price dates or history. All recommendations here are PROPOSED. EXPLICIT_USER_DECISION 2026-09-10: propose current holdings first and keep history/performance gated. This is discovery direction, not architecture acceptance or sprint selection.
- **Missing evidence or decision:** Exact approved AMFI scheme-code/ISIN/Direct-plan-option mapping for owned positions; effective date, total-cost vs average-price meaning, and correction policy.
- **Options and tradeoffs:** Current-position evidence first needs fewer source events; reconstructing lots/performance first requires dated investment transactions and a cost method.
- **Smallest proposed boundary:** PROPOSED: Define one native-INR Direct-plan position snapshot with units, source/user authority, observation/effective-date distinction and typed cost-evidence meaning. Keep provider pricing replaceable and separate.
- **Scope and exclusions:** Exclude Regular-plan substitution, fuzzy matching, inferred lots, performance and reporting FX.
- **Dependencies and decisions:** FW-P3-23 identity, FW-P3-28 correction semantics; FW-P3-27 only when fetched pricing is included.
- **ADR and migration implications:** Investment identity and owned-position ADR required; likely additive persistence and hydration work after approval, no migration number allocated.
- **Falsifiable future acceptance / independent oracle:** Verify exact configured code+ISIN+plan/option and independent unit total; mismatched or missing mapping is unavailable, never substituted. Future implementation must compare against independently reviewed genuine source/user position facts. Preserve source-observed Decimal units, price/NAV and rates at independently evidenced scale; reject malformed/out-of-contract precision only under an approved field contract. Apply explicitly versioned rounding only to final derived currency Money, then enforce its minor-unit scale. Prove SQLite/In-Memory parity where persisted, zero accepted residue on rejection, reopen/hydration and provenance. Absent authentic cases remain uncertified; do not manufacture statement graphs.
- **Invalidators / revalidation trigger:** A source showing distributions, unit-class distinctions, unsettled trades or a different cost interpretation invalidates a flat snapshot that hides them.
- **Personal-v1 relevance:** PROPOSED personal-v1 relevance: useful to replace current owner-maintained investment work; Chat must classify the exact adopted boundary before certification.
- **Queue and roadmap recommendation:** Preserve current status and priority; add dated discovery link and exact remaining gate. Prepared Sprint 98: Scope split/replanning decision required before execution; do not change numbering.
- **Evidence anchors:** [E024](#e024) — AccountType; Account baseCurrencyBalance (baseline lines 10-60); [E025](#e025) — Money.init; minorUnits (baseline lines 258-304); [E006](#e006) — WorkspaceRepository, TransactionRepository, CategoryRepository, AccountRepository, CardRepository, ImportSessionRepository and ConfirmedImportRepository; provider container (baseline lines 201-340); [E005](#e005) — inactive exchange_rates schema capacity (baseline lines 233-248); [E027](#e027) — ADR-008, ADR-033, ADR-045, ADR-046 (baseline lines 269-294;2178-2395;5680-5710;5920-6090).

### FW-P3-27 — Optional Market-Data Refresh

**Baseline:** P3; Research. **Disposition:** `NEEDS_SOURCE_OR_USER_DECISION`. **Result:** `SUBSTANTIVE_RESULT`. **Research owner:** Astra primary.
**Examined ref/date:** `adcf83f52d309ddac18d95f0321d0c0f6120dd29` / 2026-09-10. **Delta at `ffddebc6e12405c812a36855ab6212e973cff5ba`:** `STILL_VALID` — Cited input hashes unchanged.

**Recorded queue dependencies/gates at baseline:** **Depends on:** verified/user-owned holding identity and units; provider-specific access/terms and retrieval-contract qualification; offline-first cache/freshness policy **Reported technical discovery — 2026-09-09:** bounded read-only discovery captured and anonymously replayed the productive public FE request: The productive response resolved exactly one row for each configured code. Relevant discovered fields include `Common.FundCode_Customtable`, `Common.Name`, `Price.Bid.Amount`, `Price.Bid.Currency`, `Price.Currency_UnitLevel` and `Price.Change_`. Raw provider price values may carry more precision than rendered page text; preserve exact Decimal source values rather than round-tripping through display formatting. For the selected funds, provider `PriceDate`, `NAVDate` and `Time` were not established as populated source dates. Durable semantics therefore remain **`PRICE_DATE_NOT_ESTABLISHED`** unless later provider evidence proves otherwise. Fetch/observation time is provenance, not a valuation date, and any global performance date is not silently reused as the price date. The exact period represented by the provider's Change-% field is also `NOT_ESTABLISHED` until source documentation proves it. Technical retrieval is proven as reported discovery, but the current provider wording does **not** establish permission for automated persistent cached use. Provider terms/access and the exact durable-cache boundary remain implementation gates. Do not claim provider permission beyond evidence. **Reported technical discovery — 2026-09-09:** AMFI's current publication candidate is: There is **no fixed once-per-Qatar-day refresh architecture**. Each approved provider adapter must define its own freshness policy based on provider/source semantics and product use. - refresh asynchronously when the relevant view/workflow opens and the observation is stale under that provider's accepted policy; - no prior success plus unavailable source is `UNAVAILABLE`, not Money zero; - provenance retains provider/source identity, exact source value, source timestamp/date where actually supplied, fetch timestamp separately, parser/adapter-contract version and source-response digest; - provider-specific TTL/refresh numbers remain implementation-policy candidates until accepted, not architecture frozen by this planning document.
**Original FW references:** `FW-P3-20`, `FW-P3-29`

- **Question:** Which market-data gaps remain after prior live discovery?
- **Behavior / evidence classification at research ref:** VERIFIED_REPOSITORY: AccountType.investment and optional base-currency fields exist, but the inspected repository interfaces contain no holding/security/lot or reference-rate service. The exchange_rates table is historical schema capacity, not an implemented FX product. Money rejects excess currency precision. REPORTED_ONLY: the queue records successful FE/Zurich and AMFI retrieval on 2026-09-09; this campaign did not replay completed price discovery. VERIFIED_EXTERNAL 2026-09-10: AMFI terms permit personal/noncommercial use but restrict significant electronic storage; Zurich website terms restrict copying and apply separate terms to linked sites. Neither page establishes a precise FE automated-cache license. VERIFIED_EXTERNAL 2026-09-10 user-supplied FE page: footer describes valuation-date prices used for unit transactions on the following working day. The parsed overview exposed headers but no fund rows; no per-row date or Change-% period was established. Its copying notice is limited to incidental viewing/internal single copies, so automation/cache permission remains unestablished.
- **Accepted constraints:** ADR-008/033 preserve native Money and require separately approved derived rounding/conversion. ADR-046 governs every statement-dependent path. Source/user holding facts, external observations and derived values have separate authority; no invented units, cost, identifiers, price dates or history. All recommendations here are PROPOSED.
- **Missing evidence or decision:** FE per-row source valuation date and Change-% period; explicit automated retrieval/persistent-cache permission under the linked FE terms; AMFI precise automated-cache scope; provider-specific freshness/fallback and rounding contracts. The footer clarifies date semantics but does not supply the missing actual price dates.
- **Options and tradeoffs:** Manual dated observation preserves explicit provenance until terms/date evidence exists; automated fetching needs separate provider qualification. An unknown price date can remain explicitly unknown only if the adopted valuation policy permits it.
- **Smallest proposed boundary:** PROPOSED: Retain exact Decimal price and source currency outside currency-minor-unit Money; retain source date only when supplied, fetch time separately, adapter version and response digest. Cache last success and expose stale/unavailable without zero.
- **Scope and exclusions:** No price-as-ownership, global performance date as price date, inferred change period, background-polling mandate or fixed once-per-Qatar-day policy.
- **Dependencies and decisions:** FW-P3-20/21/29 owned identity and units; provider permission and rounding decisions.
- **ADR and migration implications:** Valuation/provenance ADR; additive observation storage likely; existing exchange_rates schema is not an investment-price store.
- **Falsifiable future acceptance / independent oracle:** Independent exact identity/value/date projection; cache failure preserves prior success; no-success failure is unavailable; validate provider rights and accepted time policy before production use. Future implementation must compare against independently reviewed genuine source/user position facts. Preserve source-observed Decimal units, price/NAV and rates at independently evidenced scale; reject malformed/out-of-contract precision only under an approved field contract. Apply explicitly versioned rounding only to final derived currency Money, then enforce its minor-unit scale. Prove SQLite/In-Memory parity where persisted, zero accepted residue on rejection, reopen/hydration and provenance. Absent authentic cases remain uncertified; do not manufacture statement graphs.
- **Invalidators / revalidation trigger:** Provider fields/terms/feed format or observed identity cardinality change; rounding price to displayed cents changes valuation. **Recorded queue dependencies/gates at baseline:** **Depends on:** an accepted general derived-FX architecture, provider/attribution decision, provider-specific freshness/fallback policy and provenance-bearing derived-value contract using one general QAR→INR reference leg and one fetched general QAR→USD reference leg. For one accepted source snapshot: Preserve both raw source legs, their units/orientation, provider/source timestamp, fetch timestamp separately, provider/source identity, source-response digest and derivation algorithm/version. Native `Money` remains authoritative and is never overwritten by conversion. **Technically qualified provider candidate, not selected architecture:** `ExchangeRate-API` via `https://open.er-api.com/v6/latest/QAR` can provide QAR-based INR and USD legs in one dated snapshot and documents end-use caching with attribution. The user selected the **conversion route**, not a permanent provider. Future implementation discovery/ADR must still approve provider choice, attribution, freshness, fallback and durable-storage policy. ExchangeRate-API is neither the unique allowed provider nor already implemented architecture. General refresh follows provider-specific freshness policy: use cached success first, optionally refresh asynchronously when a relevant reporting/valuation view opens and the observation is stale, allow explicit Refresh where approved, retain stale success after failure, never turn missing data into zero and do not require background polling. Provisional discovery TTL values are not frozen architecture.

Apply explicitly versioned rounding only to final derived currency Money, then enforce its minor-unit scale. - **Question:** When is currency-aware analytics/net worth meaningful?
- **Behavior / evidence classification at research ref:** VERIFIED_REPOSITORY: AccountType.investment and optional base-currency fields exist, but the inspected repository interfaces contain no holding/security/lot or reference-rate service. The exchange_rates table is historical schema capacity, not an implemented FX product. Money rejects excess currency precision. VERIFIED_LOCAL: workbook G46:H47 converts totals through present spreadsheet reference formulas, including retired haircuts. Those computed totals are historical workflow evidence, not a LedgerForge financial oracle.
- **Accepted constraints:** ADR-008/033 preserve native Money and require separately approved derived rounding/conversion. ADR-046 governs every statement-dependent path. Source/user holding facts, external observations and derived values have separate authority; no invented units, cost, identifiers, price dates or history. All recommendations here are PROPOSED.
- **Missing evidence or decision:** Accepted analytics/holding membership, valuation and rate as-of policy, missing-data aggregation and independent reconciliation scope.
- **Options and tradeoffs:** Complete native-currency reports can precede a mixed-currency view; a single total needs all component dates and values to be acceptable.
- **Smallest proposed boundary:** PROPOSED: Expose a completeness ledger for each derived total and retain native amount, price observation and both FX legs as separate inspectable facts.
- **Scope and exclusions:** No zero substitute, interpolated unobserved history, mixed dates labelled one as-of result or deprecated workbook haircuts.

### FW-P3-36 — Verified Backup, Restore and Disaster Recovery

**Baseline:** P3; Ready for discovery. **Disposition:** `DISCOVER_NOW`. **Result:** `SUBSTANTIVE_RESULT`. **Research owner:** Astra primary.
**Examined ref/date:** `adcf83f52d309ddac18d95f0321d0c0f6120dd29` / 2026-09-10. **Delta at `ffddebc6e12405c812a36855ab6212e973cff5ba`:** `STILL_VALID` — Cited input hashes unchanged.

**Recorded queue dependencies/gates at baseline:** **Implementation dependency:** Approval of the user-facing backup contents, integrity, restore-target, compatibility and confirmation contract. Discovery must distinguish the existing DEBUG lifecycle backup from a production user-owned backup product and ensure restore cannot overwrite unique/newer data without explicit review. A copied file alone is not a trustworthy backup or restore drill.
**Original FW references:** `FW-P0-26`

- **Question:** How does user-owned backup differ from DEBUG lifecycle backup?
- **Behavior / evidence classification at research ref:** VERIFIED_REPOSITORY: SQLiteDatabase uses the online backup API and checked checkpoint/close exists. DEBUG createAndVerifyBackup checks authorized target, migration chain, identifier schema and required tables. It does not establish a user-facing contents manifest, complete integrity guarantee or safe arbitrary restore target.
- **Accepted constraints:** The app remains private, local and explicit. Accepted financial history, native Money, order, multiplicity and provenance cannot be silently rewritten. ADR-046 authentic-input rules apply to financial cases; migration identities remain append-only. Discovery grants no implementation or acceptance.
- **Missing evidence or decision:** Approved contents/exclusions, backup identity/digest and integrity depth, supported versions, safe restore target, explicit replacement confirmation and recovery instructions. An independent user restore drill is absent.
- **Options and tradeoffs:** A consistent SQLite snapshot can serve one local workspace without exporting credentials; restoring into a new isolated target is easier to verify than replacing an open active database.
- **Smallest proposed boundary:** PROPOSED: Create a user-owned snapshot plus version/contents/digest manifest, verify it independently, preview compatibility and restore to a separate target before any explicitly confirmed activation. Preserve the original/newer target and last verified backup until success is proven.
- **Scope and exclusions:** No live-file-only copy, same-file restore, overwrite of unique/newer data without review, automatic schema downgrade/repair or assuming DEBUG backup equals product recovery. Credential and retained-source inclusion remain explicit decisions.
- **Dependencies and decisions:** FW-P3-36 implementation contract; existing canonical lifecycle; shared close/concurrency changes stay owned by84-87; FW-P0-26 requires adopted restore evidence.
- **ADR and migration implications:** Backup/restore lifecycle and privacy architecture decision needed. No migration solely for snapshotting; a manifest format needs its own version policy, no migration number allocated.
- **Falsifiable future acceptance / independent oracle:** Independently verify manifest digest, migration identity, SQLite integrity and required relationship/semantic scope; restore in isolation, compare genuine source-backed counts/identities/Money/provenance, close/reopen/hydrate, and prove interruptions/cancellation/corruption preserve originals and fail closed.
- **Invalidators / revalidation trigger:** Schema/provider/lifecycle change, unaccounted retained data, cross-device credential dependency or snapshot concurrency behavior changes invalidate design.
- **Personal-v1 relevance:** Explicitly required recoverability input to personal-v1; priority remains unchanged.
- **Queue and roadmap recommendation:** Retain exact current ID, priority and status; link this result and remaining named gate. No sprint selected or reordered.

- **Options and tradeoffs:** OS volume protection may meet some device-loss needs; app-managed DB/package encryption introduces independent key loss, unlock and migration obligations. Select only after the owner chooses the actual threats.
- **Smallest proposed boundary:** PROPOSED: First map financial data at rest across database, backups, exports, source retention and diagnostics, then decide the smallest encryption/recovery boundary with explicit loss-of-key consequences.
- **Scope and exclusions:** No invented FileVault-enabled claim, cryptography implementation, statement-password key reuse, hidden key escrow or encryption promise while plaintext artifacts remain unexplained.
- **Dependencies and decisions:** FW-P3-36 backup/recovery, FW-P3-17/35 exports, FW-P2-01/54 retention, credential lifecycle; workspace/sync only if adopted.
- **ADR and migration implications:** Encryption/key lifecycle ADR and explicit existing-data conversion/rollback policy; no migration or dependency chosen.
- **Falsifiable future acceptance / independent oracle:** Future qualification must inspect all approved artifacts/residue, test wrong/missing keys and interrupted conversion, restore after device loss using approved recovery evidence, and prove plaintext exposure matches the declared threat model.
- **Invalidators / revalidation trigger:** New artifact destinations, background unlock needs or required cross-device recovery change the threat model.
- **Personal-v1 relevance:** PROPOSED: optional after the adopted personal-v1 boundary unless Chat explicitly classifies it otherwise.
- **Queue and roadmap recommendation:** Retain exact current ID, priority and status; link this result and remaining named gate. No sprint selected or reordered.
